| Note |  |  | 1.29.1 | Note | DIVISION 15 - MECHANICAL (HVAC) |  |  |  |  |  |
| Note |  |  | 1.29.2 | Note | BUILDERS WORK |  |  |  |  |  |
| Note |  |  | 1.29.3 | Note | The contractor shall provide builder's work as required by the HVAC system including but not limited to the following: holes, mortises, chases and like the required in the structure for all necessary sleeves, anchors, base plates, isolation packing, holding down bolts, grouting, concrete pads except included in concrete section, painting, and structural steel. Supports in accordance with specifications in respect to all HVAC installations. |  |  |  |  |  |
| Note |  |  | 1.29.4 | Note | Duct Works |  |  |  |  |  |
